S9
Triangle Inequality for Disjoint Subpolicies

We show here that the free energy in goal-directed
navigation satisfies the triangular inequality if the trajectories
produced by the subpolicies for intermediate goals never overlap
(except at the subgoals themselves). Hence, in this case the free energy is a quasi-metric on the state space
\mathcal{S}
.
We begin with several definitions.
In the main paper we have introduced the optimal policy achieving the
minimum free energy
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}
which is defined as follows:

Definition 1
.

Given a goal state
g\in\mathcal{S}
, define
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}
via
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}(s)\vcentcolon=\arg\min_{\pi}\mathcal{F}^{\pi}_{g}(s)
for all
s\in\mathcal{S}
.

For this section it will be useful to define also the set of all states visited by an agent using a policy.

Definition 2
.

Given a policy
\pi
and a state
s\in\mathcal{S}
, define
\mathcal{S}_{\pi(s)}
as
the set containing all the states eventually visited by an agent
with probability larger than 0 when starting in state
s
and then
following the policy
\pi
. We include the starting state in

\mathcal{S}_{\pi(s)}
, i.e.
s\in\mathcal{S}_{\pi(s)}
.

In the Discussion, we have seen that, to form a proper quasi-metric on the state space
\mathcal{S}
, the free energy
\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}}_{g}(s)
from
s
to
g
must satisfy the following triangular inequality

\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}}_{g}(s)\leq\mathcal{F}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}_{s^{\prime}}(s)+\mathcal{F}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}}_{g}(s^{\prime}),

(xlii)

where
s
is the agent’s starting state,
g
is the goal state and

s^{\prime}
any interim subgoal. The optimal policy
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}
directly brings
the agent from
s
to
g
without constraining possible interim
states. In contrast, when composing the optimal subpolicies

{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}}
, the agent firstly moves from
s
to
s^{\prime}

(using
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}
) and then moves from
s^{\prime}
to
g
(via
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}}
).
We remind the reader that underlying the individual free energy terms
in (
xlii
) there are actually distinct MDPs (in our
formalism goals are modelled as absorbing states, see main text for
details); we then hierarchically combine the two policies when considering the composition of the two terms in the right hand side of the inequality. We have already shown with a counterexample that the triangular inequality (
xlii
), in its general form, does not hold (main text, (
11
)). In this section, we prove the triangular inequality when the subpolicies are
essentially disjoint
, i.e., if starting at
s
, the trajectories induced by
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}
and those by
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}}
only intersect trivially at

(namely, in the case of

as goal of the first policy, in the case of

as starting state of the second policy.)

We proceed now by proving the following theorem:

Theorem 1
.

Let
s\neq g
,
s,g\in\mathcal{S}
be given. Then, if for all
s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}
we have

\mathcal{S}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}(s)}\bigcap\mathcal{S}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscrip , then

\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}}_{g}(s)\leq\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\sc Proof
For
s^{\prime}=g
the theorem can be easily proven, since we
have
\mathcal{F}_{g}(g)=0
, then
\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}}_{g}(s)=\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}}_{s^{\prime}}(s)+0=\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}}_{g}(s)
, which fulfils
the triangular inequality, and analogously for
s^{\prime}=s
. Therefore,
assume
s\neq s^{\prime}\neq g
and we employ proof by contradiction. We will show that, if there exists a
s^{\prime}\neq g
violating this condition (i.e. for which the inequality is reversed and strict), then we can glue the subpolicies
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}}
together in a way such that the resulting new policy

achieves a smaller overall free energy than the original optimal policy
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}
, leading to a contradiction.

Formally, assume that there exists

, with

, for which

. Then, construct a new policy
\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}
, via:

\forall q\in\mathcal{S}:\;\;\;\hat{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}}(a|q)\vcentcolon=\begin{cases}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\rais {\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}}(a|q)&\text{if Note that, similar to
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}
, also
\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}
can be used by an
agent to reach the goal
g
from the initial state
s
. Furthermore,
since we assumed
\mathcal{S}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}(s)}\bigcap\mathcal{S}_{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscrip ,
\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}
is well defined for each state on the possible
trajectories, with no ambiguity as to which subpolicy is to be used.
We can then compute the free energy
\mathcal{F}^{\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}}_{g}(s)
as
follows (superscripts indicate whether states and actions belong to
the first or second section of the trajectory:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}}_{g}(s)=\sum_{(a_{0}^{1},s_{1}^{1},a_{1}^{1},\dots,a_{0}^{2},s_{1}^{2},a_{1}^{2},\dots)}p(a_{0}^{1},s_{1}^{1},a_{1}^{1},\dots,s^{\prime},a_{0}^{2},s_{1}^{2},a_{1}^{2},\dots,g|s)\cdot

(xliii)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\frac{1}{\beta}\log\frac{\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}(a_{0}^{1}|s)}{\widehat{p}(a_{0}^{1})}-r(s_{1}^{1},s,a_{0}^{1})+\frac{1}{\beta}\log\frac{\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}(a_{1}^{1}|s_{1}^{1})}{\widehat{p}(a_{1}^{1})}-\dots

\displaystyle\dots+\frac{1}{\beta}\log\frac{\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}(a_{0}^{2}|s^{\prime})}{\widehat{p}(a_{0}^{2})}-r(s_{1}^{2},s^{\prime},a_{0}^{2})+\frac{1}{\beta}\log\frac{\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}(a_{1}^{2}|s_{1}^{2})}{\widehat{p}(a_{1}^{2})}-\dots\Big)=

\displaystyle=\sum_{(a_{0}^{1},s_{1}^{1},a_{1}^{1},\dots,a_{0}^{2},s_{1}^{2},a_{1}^{2},\dots)}p(a_{0}^{1},s_{1}^{1},a_{1}^{1},\dots,s^{\prime},a_{0}^{2},s_{1}^{2},a_{1}^{2},\dots,g|s)\cdot

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\frac{1}{\beta}\log\frac{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}(a_{0}^{1}|s)}{\widehat{p}(a_{0}^{1})}-r(s_{1}^{1},s,a_{0}^{1})+\frac{1}{\beta}\log\frac{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptsty where in the last equation we substituted
\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}
by
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}

or
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}}
as per the definition of
\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}
. Now, we can use the linearity of the expected value, split the sum in (
xliii
) and marginalizing away the superfluous variables to obtain the individual free energies
\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}}_{s^{\prime}}(s)
and
\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}}}_{g}(s^{\prime})
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}}_{g}(s)=

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\sum_{(a_{0}^{1},s_{1}^{1},a_{1}^{1},\dots)}p(a_{0}^{1},s_{1}^{1},a_{1}^{1},\dots,s^{\prime}|s)\left(\frac{1}{\beta}\log\frac{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{687715}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.575pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.1025pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}(a_{0}^{1}|s)}{\widehat{p}(a_{0}^{1})}-r(s_{1}^{1},s,a_{0}^{1})+\frac{1}{\beta}\log\frac{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{358144}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{226176}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\t \displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.125pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66664pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327679}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.78749pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.66666pt}{\resizebox{558269}{327680}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5625pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}}_{s^{\prime}}(s)+\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{442473}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{ The last inequality directly comes from our main assumption of
violating the triangular inequality. In equation
(
xliv
) we have proven that

\mathcal{F}^{\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}}_{g}(s)<\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{406740}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{406740}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347061}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347060}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{250269}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{250269}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187816}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}}_{g}(s)
; i.e. when moving from

s
to
g
via
s^{\prime}
and the composite policy
\hat{\pi}_{\mathcal{F}}
, the free energy is smaller than the free
energy obtained using
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{406740}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{506045}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{212079}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{212079}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}
. But
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{347062}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{406740}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\pi$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{506045}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pi$}}}}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{212079}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{212079}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{187817}{196608}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{F}$}}}}}}}
was assumed optimal, hence
the assumption that the triangle inequality is violated leads to a
contradiction. It therefore must hold and the proof is established.

\square
